Middlesbrough boss Kim Hellberg breaks silence on Crystal Palace links

📅 2026-11-10  |  Read Source →

Kim Hellberg is continuing to earn admirers as Middlesbrough's pursuit of promotion to the Premier League toils on, and with new interest emerging from Crystal Palace ahead of Pierre Sage's increasingly-inevitable sacking, the Swede has now made his thoughts clear on a potential switch to Selhurst Park.

Middlesbrough will fancy their chances of successfully avenging last season's heartbreaking last-grasp play-off final defeat to Hull City after enjoying a strong start to the opening months of the 2026/27 Championship campaign, which has seen Hellberg's side lock down second-place heading into the November international break with eight victories, five draws and just two defeats from 15 fixtures to date.

Level on points with both league leaders Birmingham City and third-placed Stoke, Middlesbrough have often appeared the team to beat in the second-tier thus far and will intend on picking up where they left off once they return to action in two weeks' time with a double-header against Bristol City and a fellow promotion rival in Swansea - having endured a slight dip in form during Will Lankshear's injury-enforced absence.

The Teessiders have shown that, under Hellberg's tuition, they are capable of finally ending their top-flight exile and getting over the finish line once and for all, which is exactly why the Riverside Stadium faithful will want nothing more than recent links to Crystal Palace to go away.

Interestingly, though, Hellberg himself has now broken his silence on the speculation in a crucial new development.

Hellberg's exploits on Teesside appear to be gaining wider attention, and a report from Sky Sports on Sunday evening named the hugely-popular 38-year-old as one of seven candidates on Crystal Palace's managerial radar alongside the likes of Sean Dyche, Frank Lampard, Vitor Matos of Swansea and leading contender Thomas Frank.

This update, of course, comes amid the impending sacking of Pierre Sage, which is yet to be formally finalised but seemingly represents an inevitability after the Frenchman left the Eagles rooted to the foot of the top-flight table with just two wins from his opening ten encounters heading into the latest two-week window.

Hellberg has publicly spoken of his dream to manage in the Premier League, but that's precisely where Middlesbrough could be headed on his watch and the Swedish boss has now reacted to links with the likely-soon-to-be-vacancy at Selhurst Park.

Having only been appointed at Boro just under twelve months ago, Hellberg discussed the reported interest with Swedish outlet Fotbollskanalen and reiterated his desire to focus on the job at hand with the Championship promotion hopefuls.

Birmingham City and Middlesbrough are both plotting shock moves for Leeds United-linked Lorient star Arthur Avom, Football League World understands

When asked how he feels about speculation linking his services with Palace, he explained: "Absolutely nothing, absolutely nothing.

"It's always nice to be on lists like that, if that's the case - it shows you've done something right. But I think it's mostly just talk, and for me it's all about doing a good job here.

"We actually have a chance to get promoted this year. We feel that there is a chance, but then it's clear that it will be difficult.

"We are one of five, six teams, maybe not the biggest favourites, but behind them I think we have a good chance.

"My focus is here and hopefully we can get up, that would have been fantastic of course."

It's abundantly clear that the entirety of Hellberg's focus is on the task at hand, which is getting Boro back to the promised land at the second time of asking - and they've got a real strong shot of doing just that on his watch.

For a head coach of Hellberg's quality - and we scarcely see that in the Championship, truth be told - it could be easy to be swayed by the opportunity to manage at the highest level.

However, Middlesbrough's own project is attractive, with Steve Gibson rewarding Hellberg with no shortage of financial backing to bolster Boro's squad, and they've only strengthened after sanctioning the big-money departure of Hayden Hackney to Everton over the summer.

Indeed, Boro have one of the Championship's strongest and deepest sides, and from defence to attack, stars such as Adilson Malanda, Aidan Morris and most notably the aforementioned Lankshear are all long-term assets with serious future resale value.

Hellberg has a gifted and high-ceiling squad at his disposal, and missing out on promotion with what he has would undoubtedly represent a failure.

Swipe on the pitch or use the pad to move.

That said, there's no better coach to finally return Boro to the big time and, having only appointed Hellberg after Rob Edwards' contentious and ultimately ill-fated decision to jump ship for Wolves merely months after arriving in the North East, they'll only be pleased and relieved in equal measure to see much more loyalty, dedication and focus from the sought-after Swede.

Make no mistake about it, Middlesbrough won't find a finer opportunity to end their promotion hoodoo as Hellberg continues to kick on with one of the strongest Boro squads we've seen in recent history.

Albeit expensively-assembled, there's no denying the vast quality across Boro's squad. Radek Vitek between the sticks and the likes of Callum Brittain, Neto Borges, Malanda and Ash Phillips provides a second-tier backline stiffer than most, even if Hellberg's emphasis on committing bodies forward and dominating possession in attacking thirds can see the Teessiders occasionally exploited in quick counter-transitions.

You won't find many stronger and more multi-functional midfielders at this level than Middlesbrough's, though, which features Sebastian Berhalter, Riley McGree and Morris - and even versatile French defender Malanda.

The attacking line is just as impressive, and when Hellberg can call upon the joint double-act brilliance of Amario Cozier-Duberry out wide and top-scorer Lankshear alongside Tommy Conway and Jeremy Sarmiento, it's no surprise that only two sides are averaging more goals a game.

Middlesbrough fans, how relieved are you by Kim Hellberg's reaction to Crystal Palace? Are you worried at all that the Swede could leave before the job is done? Let us know your thoughts in the comments below!

Middlesbrough have serious, well-stocked quality in almost every area of the pitch and are a joy to watch at full throttle, and Hellberg himself only knows that all-too-well and won't be willing to part with this talented and exciting squad easily.

Boro, in truth, really should have held their nerve to seal a top-two finish last term, but the opportunity for promotion is even greater now with an improved squad and a route back to the top-flight by the time May rolls around may be exactly what's required to ensure Hellberg's long-term future remains at the Riverside.

Siemens to buy chip debugging specialist Lauterbach to grow its EDA unit

📅 2026-11-10  |  Read Source →

The German firm makes TRACE32 tools that help engineers find software bugs in chips before they are built.

Lauterbach’s TRACE32 debug and trace tools

Siemens has agreed to buy Lauterbach, a German company that makes tools for finding and fixing bugs in chips and the software that runs on them, but the price was not disclosed.

Founded in 1979 by brothers Lothar and Stephan Lauterbach, the company is based in Höhenkirchen, near Munich. It employs about 170 people and made around €60m in revenue last year.

Lauterbach is best known for TRACE32, a range of debugging and tracing tools. Engineers use them to see what is happening inside a chip as its software runs, and to track down errors. The company has more than 1,200 customers, mostly in the car, chip and telecoms industries.

The deal will become part of Siemens’ electronic design automation (EDA) business, which sells software for designing and testing chips. Siemens built that business mainly through its purchase of Mentor Graphics in 2017.

Siemens already sells hardware systems that imitate a chip before it is made, so that designers can test it early. By adding Lauterbach’s tools, it wants to let customers check the chip and its software together. As a result, they could start writing and testing software before the physical chip exists, and find problems sooner.

This matters more as chips and software become harder to separate. Cars, for example, are increasingly controlled by software, while AI and connected devices need chips and code designed side by side.

Cedrik Neike, who heads Siemens’ Digital Industries division, said semiconductors are a core part of the company’s technology strategy. The deal, he said, would let customers test and improve their products before the chip is made.

“With Lauterbach, we are further extending our industry-leading EDA portfolio for semiconductor design by bringing together our premier hardware-assisted verification portfolio with its industry-leading software debugging technologies,” said Cedrik Neike, Member of the Managing Board of Siemens AG and President of Digital Industries.

For its part, Lauterbach said its customer focus would stay the same.

“Our commitment to keeping our customers at the center of everything we do remains unchanged,” said Stephan Lauterbach, the company’s co-founder and chief technology officer.

He and Thomas Ullmann will stay on at Siemens once the deal is completed. Siemens also expects the deal to bring significant extra sales.

The acquisition is expected to close in the first half of 2027, subject to regulatory approval. Besides Germany, Lauterbach has offices in the UK, Italy, France, Tunisia, the US, Japan and China.

The deal adds to Siemens’ growing push into chips and industrial AI. Elsewhere, the company has been testing humanoid robots with Nvidia at its own factory in Erlangen.

Only three teams in Europe have scored more than Roma

📅 2026-11-10  |  Read Source →

Roma have had an extraordinary offensive output this season: they’ve already scored 31 goals in just 11 Serie A games, the best performance in the Giallorossi’s history at this point in the season.

It’s also a record for Gasperini: his most prolific team on matchday 11 was Atalanta in 2019-20 with 30 goals.

Roma are now averaging 2.82 goals per game. Their driving force is Donyell Malen, who has scored eight times.

Roma, when they get past their opponents this season, want to win big. In Serie A, they’ve scored at least three goals against Udinese (5), Fiorentina (4), Lecce (4), Cagliari (3), Napoli (3, drawing), and Sassuolo (3).

As La Gazzetta dello Sport reports, in the top five leagues, only Bayern Munich (3.22), Leipzig (3), and Barcelona (4) have done better than Gasperini’s team (2.82 goals).

All the other European big clubs are behind the Giallorossi: from PSG (2.2 goals per game) to Real Madrid (2.58). Roma’s performance is extraordinary, with two key factors standing out: Malen’s goals (8) and the ability of almost every player to beat opposing goalkeepers (12 different ones in the league).

Bitcoin CPI Reaction: 10 of 13 Cool Inflation Reports Triggered an Hourly Gain – Can Inflation Data Reverse the Downtrend?

📅 2026-11-10  |  Read Source →

Bitcoin CPI reaction: October CPI lands at 8:30 a.m. ET, core seen at 0.22%. Cool prints lifted BTC in the first hour 10 times out of 13.

The U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics publishes October’s consumer price index (CPI) on Tuesday, November 10, at 8:30 a.m. in New York, or 13:30 UTC. The Bitcoin CPI reaction that follows could set the tone for the week. The CPI is the main U.S. measure of inflation, and it will shape the Federal Reserve’s next rate decision.

Economists expect core prices, which leave out food and energy, to have risen about 0.22% in the month. That would be a slowdown from September’s 0.33%.

Economists expect growth in the core CPI to have moderated in October to around 0.22%, though the full range of estimates is broad enough to round down to 0.1% or round up to 0.3%.

Due to the government shutdown in Oct 2025, there's no tidy year-earlier figure. pic.twitter.com/CFM7ikw8fO

— Nick Timiraos (@NickTimiraos) November 9, 2026

Bitcoin trades near $74,400, still close to Sunday’s two-month low of $73,510.

CPI days move crypto. We checked Bitcoin’s first hour after the last 24 releases. When core inflation came in at 0.2% or lower, BTC rose in that hour 10 times out of 13. When it came in at 0.3% or higher, it rose only 4 times out of 10.

So which way will today’s number push Bitcoin?

The forecasts point to a calmer month. Seventeen banks and research firms surveyed by The Wall Street Journal see a median rise of 0.25% for headline CPI and 0.22% for core. Journal reporter Nick Timiraos posted the table on X.

That spread matters. The government reports CPI rounded to one decimal place. With forecasts this spread out, the core reading could print anywhere from 0.1% to 0.3%. A 0.3% would match September’s pace and read as inflation that is not cooling.

The yearly comparison will also be messy. The government shutdown in October 2025 meant no CPI was published for that month, so there is no clean year-over-year figure.

Market Intelligence: Crypto Analyst Predicts Best Low-Cap Crypto

This is the last CPI report the Federal Reserve will see before its December 8-9 meeting. Traders already expect a rate hike: on Tuesday morning, Polymarket gives an 86.5% chance of a 0.25-point increase and 11.5% for no change.

Higher rates tend to weigh on Bitcoin. They make cash and bonds pay more, which pulls money away from assets that pay no interest, like crypto.

A cool reading could shave those odds and give Bitcoin room to bounce. A hot one would lock in the hike.

DISCOVER: Fed Meeting Today: Traders Price a 95% Hold and a 79% December Hike. What Does It Mean for Crypto?

We measured the Bitcoin CPI reaction on Binance. We took the 24 CPI reports published since October 2024 and measured Bitcoin’s move in the hour after each 8:30 a.m. release.

The pattern is a lean, not a rule. The biggest drop came on February 12, 2025, when core rose 0.4% and BTC lost 1.9% in an hour. Last month’s 0.3% reading cost it 1.4%.

So far it has. BTC trades at $74,398 on Binance, about 1.2% above Sunday’s low.

On the daily chart, Bitcoin sits below both of its exponential moving averages (EMAs), averages that weight recent prices more. The 200-day EMA is at $76,481 and the 50-day EMA at $79,128.

For anyone tracking the Bitcoin CPI reaction, the first hour after 8:30 a.m. is the one to watch. In 20 of the last 24 reports, Bitcoin ended the next 24 hours moving in the same direction as in that first hour.

World of Warcraft: Forever might live officially on PC, but people are playing it on phones and handhelds already

📅 2026-11-10  |  Read Source →

If you're a World of Warcraft: Forever player you're almost certainly having your free time drained as you chain yourself to your desktop PC to play the game, or maybe a laptop. But for a small selection of true enthusiasts, the game is already working on mobile and handheld devices.

One such person showing off WoW: Forever on the go is none other than director of publishing at Larian, Michael Douse, who took to Twitter to show off the MMO being played on an AYN Thor device. For those who don't know, this is a fancy android handheld console, a sort of beefed up DS for the modern handheld aficionado.

"Arguably too many layers of abstraction," writes Douse. In the picture he posted, you can see the player character viewable on the Thor's top screen, while the map, text chat, skill UI, and buffs viewable on the bottom. It looks pretty cool, and is made possible due to JReese702's Github mod you can download at your leisure.

Arguably too many layers of abstraction pic.twitter.com/VjW0IX00Gg

But it's not the only mo-bell at the ball. Another Github WoW: Forever mod, WolkaraForever, gets the game playable on the iPhone Duo. As highlighted by CEO of esports company 100 Thieves Alex Wechsler on Twitter, the game looks to be ported pretty well to the phone, with mobile touchscreen controls viewable on both sides of the screen. Be warned though, as confirmed by Wechsler in a Twitter reply, it'll get your phone running hot.

As a father of 2 kids and always having wanted to play WOW, I’ve finally found a way 🥹 pic.twitter.com/eIVn0gn1WC

This coming alongside World of Warcraft: Forever's built-in (and very good) controller support could mean the game has real potential to spread far and wide across the vast array of different hardware out there. Blizzard has already hinted at plans of porting the game to other platforms, so who knows? Maybe we can expect an official mobile port to tread the path paved by these community-made mods.

GTA VI takes over miami with six real-life cars built by west coast customs

📅 2026-11-10  |  Read Source →

from a pink-and-purple sports coupe to a police-liveried supercar, the fully drivable lineup turns fictional automotive excess into something with actual horsepower.

Rockstar Games has brought six vehicles from Grand Theft Auto VI out of the screen and onto the streets of Miami, enlisting Los Angeles-based West Coast Customs to transform fictional designs into fully drivable machines. The project arrives ahead of the game’s November 19 release, translating some of its most recognizable automotive creations into physical vehicles. From classic American lowriders to contemporary supercars, the collection recreates the exaggerated personalities that have long defined the franchise’s approach to car culture.

The six vehicles retain their fictional brand names, distinctive silhouettes and elaborate finishes, with modifications extending well beyond their exterior appearance. Each has been presented alongside its digital counterpart, revealing how closely the real builds follow their virtual references. Photographs also place the collection at recognizable Miami landmarks, including Herzog & de Meuron’s 1111 Lincoln Road parking structure and the Miami Marine Stadium. The locations connect the actual city with Vice City, Rockstar’s fictional interpretation of Florida’s coastal metropolis.

Among the six creations developed by Rockstar Games in collaboration with West Coast Customs, the Grotti Cheetah draws on the proportions of classic Italian supercars, with a low-slung body, sculpted side intakes and a naturally aspirated 4.9-liter V8 engine. The customization workshop finishes the vehicle with a gradient paint treatment, lowered suspension and custom leather upholstery. The Invetero Coquette takes a more contemporary direction, pairing a 6.2-liter V8 with a striking pink-and-purple camouflage exterior. Meanwhile, the Progen Emerus embraces track-inspired engineering, combining a twin-turbocharged 4.0-liter V8 with aerodynamic bodywork, an oversized rear wing and a bright orange interior.

American automotive culture provides the references for the Albany Buccaneer and Albany Manana. The former reinterprets a 1960s lowrider with candy-green metallic paint, gold wheels, air suspension and zebra-patterned interior details. The Manana follows a different tradition, adopting the dramatically elevated stance of a donk-style custom car, complete with oversized wheels, blue bodywork and gold accents. Completing the lineup is the Pegassi Tempesta, a V10-powered supercar dressed in the fictional Vice Beach Police Department’s black-and-white livery. Apparently, even law enforcement gets a supercar in Leonida.

The project reflects the close relationship between Grand Theft Auto’s visual identity and real-world automotive customization. Throughout the series, Rockstar Games has developed fictional manufacturers and vehicles that borrow recognizable proportions and styling cues from established brands while exaggerating their defining characteristics. Translating these designs into functional machines requires more than reproducing their shapes. Paint finishes, wheel proportions, suspension setups and interior materials become essential to preserving the identity of each vehicle beyond the screen.

The resulting collection also highlights the influence of Miami’s automotive culture on the fictional world of Vice City, from extravagant lowriders to brightly colored performance cars. By placing the vehicles in the same urban environment that inspires the game, Rockstar Games brings its digital references back to their physical origins. With Grand Theft Auto VI scheduled to launch on PlayStation 5 and Xbox Series X|S on November 19, the six builds offer an unusually tangible preview of its universe, where even the most improbable designs appear perfectly at home on the road.

customization: West Coast Customs | @westcoastcustoms
